# ADR-015/ADR-016 Coordinated Clause Traceability Registry

Version: Draft 0.1 - Phase 3A

Document Type: Clause-level forward/reverse traceability registry

Status: **DRAFT - NONCANONICAL - NOT APPROVED**

Implementation Authorization: None

Runtime Verification Status: Not authorized and not performed

## 1. Scope and enumeration rule

This registry enumerates mandatory clause units in ADR-015, ADR-016, the Entry Session Rollover Contract, Production Startup and Recovery Specification, and Diagnostic Endpoint Purity Contract. ADR-014 remains approved, governing, and unchanged.

A clause unit is a source line containing `SHALL`, `MUST`, or their negative forms, a numbered mandatory step, a coded normative table row, or a bullet introduced by a mandatory clause. One row may cover multiple grammatical modal occurrences that form one source clause; the exact source line is authoritative. Stable IDs SHALL NOT be renumbered after independent review; removed clauses are retired, never reused.

Verification IDs are future obligations, not claims that tests exist or ran. Every implementation status is `NOT AUTHORIZED / NOT IMPLEMENTED`. All five source documents and this registry remain pending independent approval; ADR-014 approval is not transferred to its draft supporting contract.

## 4. Registry validation contract

- Forward IDs SHALL be unique and SHALL equal the section 2 row count.
- Every forward row SHALL map to at least one reverse verification ID.
- Every reverse verification ID SHALL map to at least one forward row.
- Every row SHALL name an exact source section/line, owner, writer or producer, and test obligations.
- Broad document-only mapping, duplicate ownership, or an orphan test SHALL fail validation.
- The diagnostic route/call-graph manifest SHALL discover runtime route reachability only and SHALL NOT replace this registry.

## 5. Governance boundary

This registry is draft provenance and review input only. It creates no approval, canonical incorporation, implementation, runtime verification, deployment, `READY_LOCKED`, Bucket 0 completion, Bucket 1 authorization, or trading authorization. `DEBT-2026-07-17-012` and `DEBT-2026-07-17-016` remain `BLOCKING`.
